}\n    public PasswordHasher(int iterations) {\n        if (iterations < MIN_ITERATIONS || iterations > MAX_ITERATIONS) throw new IllegalArgumentException(...);\n        this.iterations = iterations;\n        this.random = new SecureRandom();\n    }\n\n    public String hash(char[] password) {\n        byte[] salt = new byte[SALT_BYTES];\n        random.nextBytes(salt);\n        byte[] dk = derive(password, salt, iterations);\n        try {\n            return SCHEME + \"$\" + PARAMETER + \"$\" + iterations + \"$\" + b64(salt) + \"$\" + b64(dk);\n        } finally { java.util.Arrays.fill(dk, (byte)0); }\n    }\n\n    public boolean verify(char[] password, String encoded) {\n        Parsed p = parse(encoded);\n        if (p == null) return false;\n        byte[] dk = derive(password, p.salt, p.iterations);  // must not throw on wrong pw\n        try { return MessageDigest.isEqual(p.digest, dk); } finally { Arrays.fill(dk,(byte)0); }\n    }\n    ...\n    public boolean looksValid(String encoded) { return parse(encoded) != null; }\n    // burn: fixed salt for non-existent users\n    public static final String TIMING_BURN_SALT = \"AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA==\"; // 16 bytes of zeros b64\n    public void burn() { derive(PUBLIC_BURN_PASSWORD, saltBytes, iterations); }\n```\nderive must not leak exceptions; wrap GeneralSecurityException in a RuntimeException (IllegalStateException) — that's a real failure, fine to throw.\n\nFor the dummy/timing path: `verify(password, encodedBurn)` where encodedBurn is a precomputed valid encoded string with the same iterations. temp.toFile().setWritable(false, false);\n            temp.toFile().setReadable(true, true);\n            temp.toFile().setWritable(true, true);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // This is the only place that is allowed to answer \"yes\" to a\n * login, so the controls that matter for a privileged portal live here:\n *\n * <ul>\n *   <li><b>No plaintext, ever.</b> The password is compared by re-deriving the stored\n *       digest; it is never stored, logged, returned, or written to the audit trail.</li>\n *   <li><b>No user enumeration.</b> Every non-success outcome shows the same message, and\n *       every path performs one real key derivation, so neither the response text nor the\n *       response time reveals whether a username exists.</li>\n *   <li><b>Bounded attempts.</b> Repeated failures lock an account for a fixed window and\n *       are recorded; a sliding global window additionally throttles credential stuffing\n *       across many usernames.</li>\n *   <li><b>Fail closed.</b> An unprovisioned store, a corrupt digest or a disabled account\n *       can never authenticate.</li>\n * </ul>\n */\npublic final class AuthenticationService {\n\n    /** Deliberately narrow: blocks injection payloads and control bytes at the boundary. */\n    private static final Pattern USERNAME_PATTERN = Pattern.compile(\"[A-Za-z0-9._@\\\\-]{1,64}\");\n\n    private final CredentialStore store;\n    private final PasswordHasher hasher;\n    private final AuthenticationConfig config;\n    private final AuditLog audit;\n    private final portal.security.Clock clock;\n    private final boolean persistStateChanges;\n\n    private final Deque<Long> recentFailures = new ArrayDeque<>();\n    private long lastFailureMillis;\n\n    public AuthenticationService(CredentialStore store, PasswordHasher hasher, AuthenticationConfig config,\n                                 AuditLog audit, portal.security.Clock clock) {\n        this(store, hasher, config, audit, clock, true);\n    }\n\n    public AuthenticationService(CredentialStore store, PasswordHasher hasher, AuthenticationConfig config,\n                                 AuditLog audit, portal.security.Clock clock, boolean persistStateChanges) {\n        if (store == null || hasher == null || config == null || audit == null || clock == null) {\n            throw new IllegalArgumentException(\"authentication service requires all collaborators\");\n        }\n        this.store = store;\n        this.hasher = hasher;\n        this.config = config;\n        this.audit = audit;\n        this.clock = clock;\n        this.persistStateChanges = persistStateChanges;\n    }\n\n    public AuthenticationConfig config() {\n        return config;\n    }\n\n    public long lastFailureMillis() {\n        return lastFailureMillis;\n    }\n\n    public synchronized int failuresInWindow() {\n        pruneFailures(clock.currentTimeMillis());\n        return recentFailures.size();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Verifies credentials.\n     *\n     * @param username account name, matched case-insensitively\n     * @param password caller-owned copy; this method does not retain it but the caller\n     *                 should still erase it when finished\n     * @return the outcome; {@link AuthResult#granted()} is true only for a real match on\n     *         an active account\n     */\n    public synchronized AuthResult authenticate(String username, char[] password) {\n        long now = clock.currentTimeMillis();\n\n        if (!isWellFormedUsername(username) || password == null || password.length == 0) {\n            // Equal work, same message: a malformed name must not be cheaper or louder\n            // than a wrong password.\n            hasher.spendSameWork(password);\n            audit.record(AuditResult.FAILURE, username, \"rejected malformed login input\");\n            return AuthResult.of(AuthResult.Status.INVALID_INPUT, AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE);\n        }\n\n        if (isThrottled(now)) {\n            long wait = secondsUntil(throttleEndsAt());\n            audit.record(AuditResult.WARNING, username,\n                    \"attempt refused, \" + recentFailures.size() + \" failures inside the throttle window\");\n            return AuthResult.retryLater(AuthResult.Status.THROTTLED,\n                    \"Too many recent sign-in attempts. Try again in \" + wait + \" seconds.\", wait);\n        }\n\n        if (store.isEmpty()) {\n            audit.record(AuditResult.WARNING, username, \"no administrator accounts are provisioned\");\n            return AuthResult.of(AuthResult.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED,\n                    \"No administrator accounts are configured yet. Run with --bootstrap-first-admin to create one.\");\n        }\n\n        String name = username.trim();\n        Optional<AdminAccount> found = store.find(name);\n        if (found.isEmpty()) {\n            hasher.spendSameWork(password);\n            registerFailure(now);\n            audit.record(AuditResult.FAILURE, name, \"no such administrator account\");\n            return AuthResult.of(AuthResult.Status.INVALID_CREDENTIALS, AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE);\n        }\n\n        AdminAccount account = found.get();\n\n        if (account.state() == AccountState.DISABLED) {\n            hasher.spendSameWork(password);\n            audit.record(AuditResult.DENIED, name, \"account is disabled\");\n            return AuthResult.of(AuthResult.Status.DISABLED, AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE);\n        }\n\n        if (account.state() == AccountState.LOCKED) {\n            if (account.lockedUntilMillis() > now) {\n                hasher.spendSameWork(password);\n                long wait = secondsUntil(account.lockedUntilMillis());\n                audit.record(AuditResult.LOCKED, name, \"locked for another \" + wait + \" seconds\");\n                return AuthResult.retryLater(AuthResult.Status.LOCKED, AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE, wait);\n            }\n            account = account.withState(AccountState.ACTIVE, now);\n            store.update(account);\n            persist();\n            audit.record(AuditResult.SUCCESS, name, \"temporary lock expired\");\n        }\n\n        boolean matches = hasher.verify(password, account.passwordDigest());\n        if (matches) {\n            AdminAccount cleared = account.withSuccessfulAttempt(now);\n            store.update(cleared);\n            persist();\n            recentFailures.clear();\n            AdminPrincipal principal = new AdminPrincipal(cleared.username(), cleared.role());\n            audit.record(AuditResult.SUCCESS, name, \"credentials verified for role \" + cleared.role());\n            return AuthResult.success(principal);\n        }\n\n        int failures = account.consecutiveFailures() + 1;\n        long lockedUntil = 0L;\n        if (failures >= config.maxFailedAttempts()) {\n            lockedUntil = now + config.lockDurationMillis();\n        }\n        store.update(account.withFailedAttempt(failures, lockedUntil, now));\n        persist();\n        registerFailure(now);\n\n        if (lockedUntil > 0) {\n            audit.record(AuditResult.LOCKED, name, \"locked after \" + failures + \" consecutive failures\");\n        } else {\n            audit.record(AuditResult.FAILURE, name,\n                    \"bad credentials (\" + failures + \"/\" + config.maxFailedAttempts() + \")\");\n        }\n        return AuthResult.of(AuthResult.Status.INVALID_CREDENTIALS, AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE);\n    }\n\n    /** Clears a temporary lock. Two invariants are enforced here rather than left to\n * the operator: the last active super-administrator cannot be disabled, demoted or deleted\n * (that would lock everybody out of the portal with no way back), and any action that\n * implies compromised or changed credentials immediately revokes that user's live sessions.\n */\npublic final class AdminOperations {\n\n    private final entirely. */\n    public List<AccountView> listAccounts(String token) {\n        AdminPrincipal actor = gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_READ, token);\n        List<AccountView> views = new account.consecutiveFailures(), account.updatedAtMillis()));\n        }\n        audit.record(AuditResult.SUCCESS, actor.username(),\n                \"listed \" + views.size() + \" administrator   /** Creates an administrator. Both password entries must agree and satisfy the policy. */\n    public OperationOutcome createAdministrator(String token, String username, char[] password,\n                                                char[] passwordConfirmation, AdminRole role) {\n        AdminPrincipal actor = gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_MANAGE, token);\n        if (!AuthenticationService.isWellFormedUsername(username)) {\n            return OperationOutcome.rejected(\"Username may use letters, digits and . _ @ - and be 1-64 characters.\");\n        }\n        if (role == null) {\n            return OperationOutcome.rejected(\"A role is required.\");\n        }\n        if (store.exists(username)) {\n            return OperationOutcome.rejected(\"An administrator with that name already exists.\");\n        }\n        List<String> problems = checkPassword(password, passwordConfirmation, username);\n        if (!problems.isEmpty()) {\n            return OperationOutcome.rejected(String.join(\"; \", problems));\n        }\n\n        AdminAccount account = new AdminAccount(username.trim(), hasher.hash(password), role,\n                AccountState.ACTIVE, 0, 0L, clock.currentTimeMillis());\n        store.put(account);\n        store.save();\n        audit.record(AuditResult.SUCCESS, actor.username(),\n                \"created administrator \" + account.username() + \" with role \" + role);\n        return OperationOutcome.accepted(\"Created administrator \" + account.username() + \" as \" + role + '.');\n    }\n\n    public OperationOutcome disableAccount(String token, String username) {\n        AdminPrincipal actor = gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_MANAGE, token);\n        Optional<AdminAccount> target = store.find(username);\n        if (target.isEmpty()) {\n            return OperationOutcome.rejected(\"No such administrator account.\");\n        }\n        AdminAccount account = target.get();\n        if (isLastActiveSuperAdmin(account)) {\n            return OperationOutcome.rejected(\n                    \"Refusing to disable the only active SUPER_ADMIN; Live sessions for that account are revoked\n     * unconditionally: if the password needed resetting, tokens issued under the old one\n     * can no longer be trusted.\n     */\n    public OperationOutcome resetPassword(String token, String username, char[] newPassword,\n                                          char[] passwordConfirmation) {\n        AdminPrincipal actor = gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_MANAGE, token);\n        Optional<AdminAccount> target = store.find(username);\n        if (target.isEmpty()) {\n            return OperationOutcome.rejected(\"No such administrator account.\");\n        }\n        AdminAccount account = target.get();\n        List<String> problems = checkPassword(newPassword, OperationOutcome.accepted(\"Password updated for \" + account.username()\n                + \"; \" + revoked + \" live session(s) were revoked.\");\n    }\n\n    public OperationOutcome changeRole(String token, String username, AdminRole newRole) {\n        AdminPrincipal actor = gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_MANAGE, token);\n        Optional<AdminAccount> target = store.find(username);\n        if (target.isEmpty()) {\n            return OperationOutcome.rejected(\"No such administrator account.\");\n        }\n        if (newRole == null) {\n            return OperationOutcome.rejected(\"A role is required.\");\n        }\n        AdminAccount account = target.get();\n        if (account.role() == AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN && newRole != AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN\n sessions.activeSessions()) {\n            lines.add(\"session \" + session);\n        }\n        return lines;\n    }\n\n    public String describeRole(AdminRole role) {\n        StringBuilder out = new StringBuilder(role.name()).append(\": \");\n        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (AdminRole.Permission permission : role.permissions()) {\n            names.add(permission.description());\n        }\n        return out.append(String.join(\", \", names)).toString();\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers\n\n    private List<String> checkPassword(char[] password, char[] confirmation, String username) {\n        List<String> problems = new ArrayList<>();\n        if (password == null || password.length == 0) {\n            problems.add(\"A password is required\");\n            return problems;\n        }\n        if (confirmation == null || !java.util.Arrays.equals(password, confirmation)) {\n            problems.add(\"The two password entries do not match\");\n        }\n        for (String violation : policy.violations(new String(password), username)) {\n How many tries\n *       remain is written to the audit trail, not to the screen, because \"2 attempts\n *       remaining for user alice\" confirms that alice exists.</li>\n * </ul>\n */\npublic final class AdminConsole {\n\n    /** Client-side backstop so a stuck operator cannot spin against the lockout forever. */\n    public static final int MAX_SIGNIN_TURNS = 5;\n\n    private final AuthenticationService authentication;\n    private final SessionManager sessions;\n    private final AdminOperations operations;\n    private final AdminGate gate;\n    private final InteractiveReader reader;\n    private final PrintStream out;\n\n    private String sessionToken;\n\n    public AdminConsole(AuthenticationService authentication, SessionManager sessions,\n                        AdminOperations operations, AdminGate gate,\n                        InteractiveReader reader, PrintStream out) {\n        this.authentication = authentication;\n        this.sessions = sessions;\n        this.operations = operations;\n        this.gate = gate;\n        this.reader = reader;\n        this.out = out;\n    }\n\n    /** @return process exit code: 0 clean sign-out, 3 not provisioned, 4 too many failures */\n    public int run() {\n        printBanner();\n        for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= MAX_SIGNIN_TURNS; attempt++) {\n            AuthResult result = promptForCredentials();\n            if (result.granted()) {\n                sessionToken = sessions.issue(result.principal()).tokenId();\n                out.println();\n                out.println(\"Access granted. Signed in as \" + result.principal() + '.');\n                return menuLoop();\n            }\n            out.println(result.userMessage());\n            if (result.retryAfterSeconds() > 0) {\n                out.println(\"Retry is possible in \" + result.retryAfterSeconds() + \" seconds.\");\n            }\n            if (result.status() == AuthResult.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED) {\n                out.println(\"Nothing to sign in to yet. Re-run with --bootstrap-first-admin.\");\n                return 3;\n            }\n        }\n        out.println(\"Sign-in has been stopped for this terminal after \" + MAX_SIGNIN_TURNS + \" attempts.\");\n        return 4;\n    }\n\n    private AuthResult promptForCredentials() {\n        String username = reader.readLine(\"username: \");\n        char[] password = reader.readSecret(\"password: \");\n        try {\n            if (username == null) {\n                return AuthResult.of(AuthResult.Status.INVALID_INPUT, AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE);\n            }\n            return authentication.authenticate(username.trim(), password);\n        } finally {\n            TerminalReader.erase(password);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private int menuLoop() {\n        while (true) {\n            out.println();\n            out.println(menu());\n            String command = reader.readLine(\"admin> \");\n            if (command == null) {\n                signOut();\n                return 0;\n            }\n            command = command.trim().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n            if (command.isEmpty()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            switch (command) {\n                case \"q\":\n                case \"signout\":\n                case \"logout\":\n                    signOut();\n                    return 0;\n                case \"whoami\":\n                    whoami();\n                    break;\n                case \"1\":\n                    listAccounts();\n                    break;\n                case \"2\":\n                    createAdministrator();\n                    break;\n                case \"3\":\n                    resetPassword();\n                    break;\n                case \"4\":\n                    changeState(true);\n                    break;\n                case \"5\":\n                    changeState(false);\n                    break;\n                case \"6\":\n                    changeRole();\n                    break;\n                case \"7\":\n                    sessionsView();\n                    break;\n                case \"8\":\n                    auditView();\n                    break;\n                default:\n                    out.println(\"Unrecognised choice \\\"\" + command + \"\\\". Enter a number from the menu, \"\n                            + \"whoami, or q.\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    private String menu() {\n        return \"Administrator portal\\n\"\n                + \"  1. List administrator accounts\\n\"\n                + \"  2. Create an administrator\\n\"\n                + \"  3. Reset an administrator password\\n\"\n                + \"  4. Enable an account\\n\"\n                + \"  5. Disable an account\\n\"\n                + \"  6. Change an account role\\n\"\n                + \"  7. List / revoke live sessions\\n\"\n                + \"  8. Review recent audit events\\n\"\n                + \"  whoami  show my identity and permissions\\n\"\n                + \"  q       sign out\";\n    }\n\n    private void whoami() {\n        try {\n            AdminPrincipal me = gate.requireSignedIn(sessionToken);\n            out.println(\"signed in as \" + me.username() + \", role \" + me.role());\n            out.println(\"permissions:\");\n            for (AdminRole.Permission permission : me.permissions()) {\n                out.println(\"  - \" + permission + \" (\" + permission.description() + ')');\n            }\n        } catch (AccessDeniedException e) {\n            out.println(e.getMessage());\n            sessionToken = null;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void listAccounts() {\n        guarded(\"list accounts\", () -> {\n            List<AdminOperations.AccountView> accounts = operations.listAccounts(sessionToken);\n            if (accounts.isEmpty()) {\n                out.println(\"No administrator accounts are configured.\");\n                return;\n            }\n            for (AdminOperations.AccountView account : accounts) {\n                out.println(\"  \" + account);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private void createAdministrator() {\n        String username = reader.readLine(\"new username: \");\n        if (username == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        out.println(\"Password must be at least \" + PasswordPolicy.MIN_LENGTH\n                + \" characters and mix character classes.\");\n        char[] first = reader.readSecret(\"new password: \");\n        char[] second = reader.readSecret(\"confirm password: \");\n        AdminRole role = readRole();\n        try {\n            guarded(\"create administrator\", () -> report(operations.createAdministrator(\n                    sessionToken, username.trim(), first, second, role)));\n        } finally {\n            TerminalReader.erase(first);\n            TerminalReader.erase(second);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void resetPassword() {\n        String username = reader.readLine(\"account to reset: \");\n        if (username == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        char[] first = reader.readSecret(\"new password: \");\n        char[] second = reader.readSecret(\"confirm password: \");\n        try {\n            guarded(\"reset password\", () -> report(operations.resetPassword(\n                    sessionToken, username.trim(), first, second, second)));\n        } finally {\n            TerminalReader.erase(first);\n            TerminalReader.erase(second);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void changeState(boolean enable) {\n        String username = reader.readLine(enable ? \"account to enable: \" : \"account to disable: \");\n        if (username == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        guarded(enable ? \"enable account\" : \"disable account\", () -> report(enable\n                ? operations.enableAccount(sessionToken, username.trim())\n                : operations.disableAccount(sessionToken, username.trim())));\n    }\n\n    private void changeRole() {\n        String username = reader.readLine(\"account to change: \");\n        if (username == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        AdminRole role = readRole();\n        if (role == null) {\n            out.println(\"No role selected; nothing changed.\");\n            return;\n        }\n        guarded(\"change role\", () -> report(operations.changeRole(sessionToken, username.trim(), role)));\n    }\n\n    private void sessionsView() {\n        guarded(\"list sessions\", () -> {\n            List<String> live = operations.liveSessions(sessionToken);\n            if (live.isEmpty()) {\n                out.println(\"No live sessions.\");\n                return;\n            }\n            for (String line : live) {\n                out.println(\"  \" + line);\n            }\n            String target = reader.readLine(\"revoke all sessions for a username (blank for none): \");\n            if (target != null && !target.trim().isEmpty()) {\n                report(operations.revokeSessions(sessionToken, target.trim()));\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private void auditView() {\n        guarded(\"review audit events\", () -> {\n            List<String> events = operations.recentAuditEvents(sessionToken, 40);\n            if (events.isEmpty()) {\n                out.println(\"This audit sink does not retain history for review.\");\n                return;\n            }\n            for (String line : events) {\n                out.println(\"  \" + line);\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    private AdminRole readRole() {\n        out.println(\"roles: \" + AdminRole.ALL);\n        String entered = reader.readLine(\"role [HELPDESK]: \");\n        if (entered == null) {\n            return AdminRole.HELPDESK;\n        }\n        entered = entered.trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);\n        if (entered.isEmpty()) {\n            return AdminRole.HELPDESK;\n        }\n        try {\n            return AdminRole.valueOf(entered);\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {\n            out.println(\"Unknown role \\\"\" + entered + \"\\\"; using HELPDESK.\");\n            return AdminRole.HELPDESK;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void report(AdminOperations.OperationOutcome outcome) {\n        out.println(outcome.message());\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Runs a privileged action, converting an authorization failure into a readable line.\n     * A missing session is terminal for the menu: the token is dropped so the next command\n     * cannot keep retrying with a dead credential.\n     */\n    private void guarded(String what, Runnable action) {\n        try {\n            action.run();\n        } catch (AccessDeniedException e) {\n            out.println(\"ACCESS DENIED: \" + e.getMessage());\n            if (e.permission() == null) {\n                out.println(\"Your session is gone; commands now need a fresh sign-in.\");\n                sessionToken = null;\n            }\n        } catch (IllegalStateException e) {\n            out.println(\"COULD NOT COMPLETE \" + what + \": \" + e.getMessage());\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void signOut() {\n        if (sessionToken != null) {\n            sessions.revoke(sessionToken);\n            sessionToken = null;\n            out.println(\"Signed out.\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void printBanner() {\n        out.println(\"Administrator portal - privileged access.\");\n        out.println(\"Every sign-in attempt, success or failure, is recorded in the audit trail.\");\n        if (!reader.supportsSecretEntry()) {\n            out.println(\"NOTE: passwords will be echoed unless a terminal or \"\n                    + \"ADMIN_PORTAL_PASSWORD_FILE is available.\");\n        }\n        out.println();\n The provisioning password\n * is taken from a secret prompt, or from the file named by\n * {@code ADMIN_PORTAL_PASSWORD_FILE} for non-interactive runs, and is never read from\n * {@code argv} where {@code ps} and shell history would expose it.\n */\npublic final class Main {\n\n    /** Recognised for the single-use bootstrap password file; i < args.length; i++) {\n            String arg = args[i];\n            switch (arg) {\n                case \"--help\":\n                case \"-h\":\n                    help = true;\n                    break;\n                case \"--store\":\n                    if (i + 1 >= args.length) {\n                        out.println(\"--store needs a path.\");\n                        return 2;\n                    }\n                    storeFile = Paths.get(args[++i]);\n                    break;\n                case \"--bootstrap-first-admin\":\n                    bootstrap = true;\n                    if (i + 1 < args.length && !args[i + 1].startsWith(\"--\")) {\n                        usernameArg = args[++i];\n                    }\n                    break;\n                case \"--verify\":\n                    if (i + 1 >= args.length) {\n                        out.println(\"--verify needs a username.\");\n                        return 2;\n                    }\n                    verifyUser = args[++i];\n                    break;\n                default:\n                    out.println(\"Unrecognised option \\\"\" + arg + \"\\\". See --help.\");\n                    return 2;\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (help) {\n            out.println(usage());\n            return 0;\n        }\n\n        PortalApp app;\n        try {\n            app = PortalApp.create(storeFile);\n        } catch (IllegalStateException e) {\n            out.println(\"Cannot open the credential store: \" + e.getMessage());\n            out.println(\"Fix or remove the offending record; do not hand-edit this file.\");\n            return 5;\n        }\n\n        for (String warning : app.store().loadWarnings()) {\n            out.println(\"WARNING: \" + warning);\n        }\n\n        if (bootstrap) {\n            return bootstrap(app, usernameArg, storeFile, out);\n        }\n        if (verifyUser != null) {\n            return verify(app, verifyUser, out);\n        }\n        return app.console(new TerminalReader(out), out).run();\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Provisions the very first administrator, always as {@code SUPER_ADMIN}.\n     *\n     * <p>Refuses to run once any account exists: an unauthenticated bootstrap that could\n     * silently mint a second super-administrator would undo the point of having accounts at\n     * all. Subsequent administrators are created from inside an authenticated session.\n     */\n    static int bootstrap(PortalApp app, String requestedUsername, Path storeFile, java.io.PrintStream out) {\n        CredentialStore store = app.store();\n        if (!store.isEmpty()) {\n            out.println(\"Refusing to bootstrap: \" + store.size() + \" administrator account(s) already exist in \"\n                    + storeFile + '.');\n            out.println(\"Sign in and use the menu to add more administrators.\");\n            return 6;\n        }\n\n        InteractiveReader reader = new TerminalReader(out);\n        String username = requestedUsername;\n        while (username == null || !AuthenticationService.isWellFormedUsername(username)) {\n            if (username != null) {\n                out.println(\"Invalid username. Use letters, digits and . _ @ - (1-64 characters).\");\n            }\n            username = reader.readLine(\"new super-administrator username: \");\n            if (username == null) {\n                out.println(\"No username supplied.\");\n                return 2;\n            }\n            username = username.trim();\n        }\n\n        char[] password = readProvisioningPassword(reader, app.policy(), username, out, true);\n        if (password == null) {\n            return 2;\n        }\n        char[] confirmation = null;\n        try {\n            if (System.getenv(PASSWORD_FILE_ENV) == null) {\n                confirmation = readProvisioningPassword(reader, app.policy(), username, out, false);\n                if (confirmation == null || !Arrays.equals(password, confirmation)) {\n                    out.println(\"The two password entries do not match. Nothing was created.\");\n                    return 2;\n                }\n            }\n            AdminRole role = AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN;\n            long now = app.clock().currentTimeMillis();\n            store.put(new portal.admin.AdminAccount(username, app.hasher().hash(password), role,\n                    portal.admin.AccountState.ACTIVE, 0, 0L, now));\n            store.save();\n            out.println(\"Provisioned \" + username + \" as \" + role + \" in \" + storeFile\n                    + \" (PBKDF2-SHA256, \" + app.hasher().iterations() + \" iterations).\");\n            out.println(\"The file is readable only by this user. Back it up somewhere equally protected.\");\n            return 0;\n        } finally {\n            TerminalReader.erase(password);\n            TerminalReader.erase(confirmation);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Reads one provisioning password, repeating until it satisfies the policy. The\n     * {@code ADMIN_PORTAL_PASSWORD_FILE} path allows a single attempt - a scripted run\n     * cannot be prompted - and is read once, then left for the operator to delete.\n     */\n    private static char[] readProvisioningPassword(InteractiveReader reader, PasswordPolicy policy,\n                                                   String username, java.io.PrintStream out,\n                                                   boolean allowEnv) {\n        String fromFile = allowEnv ? System.getenv(PASSWORD_FILE_ENV) : null;\n        if (fromFile != null && !fromFile.trim().isEmpty()) {\n            Path path = Paths.get(fromFile.trim());\n            try {\n                List<String> lines = Files.readAllLines(path, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n                String value = lines.isEmpty() ? \"\" : lines.get(0).trim();\n                List<String> problems = policy.violations(value, username);\n                if (!problems.isEmpty()) {\n                    out.println(\"The password in \" + path + \" is not acceptable: \" + String.join(\"; \", problems));\n                    return null;\n                }\n                return value.toCharArray();\n            } catch (IOException e) {\n                out.println(\"Cannot read \" + path + \": \" + e.getMessage());\n                return null;\n            }\n        }\n\n        for (int attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) {\n            out.println(\"Password must be at least \" + PasswordPolicy.MIN_LENGTH\n                    + \" characters, mix character classes, and not be a common password.\");\n            char[] entered = reader.readSecret(\"password: \");\n            List<String> problems = policy.violations(new String(entered), username);\n            if (problems.isEmpty()) {\n                return entered;\n            }\n            TerminalReader.erase(entered);\n            out.println(\"Password rejected: \" + String.join(\"; \", problems) + '.');\n        }\n        out.println(\"Giving up after 3 rejected passwords. Nothing was created.\");\n        return null;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Non-interactive credential check, useful for smoke-testing a deployment. Prints the\n     * same generic message a browser would see, so it cannot be used to enumerate names.\n     *\n     * @return 0 when the credentials are valid, 1 when they are not, 3 when unprovisioned\n     */\n    static int verify(PortalApp app, String username, java.io.PrintStream out) {\n        TerminalReader reader = new TerminalReader(out);\n        char[] password = reader.readSecret(\"password: \");\n        try {\n            AuthResult result = app.authentication().authenticate(username, password);\n            if (result.granted()) {\n                out.println(\"Credentials valid for role \" + result.principal().role() + '.');\n                return 0;\n            }\n            if (result.status() == AuthResult.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED) {\n                out.println(result.userMessage());\n                return 3;\n            }\n            out.println(result.userMessage());\n            return 1;\n        } finally {\n            TerminalReader.erase(password);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static String usage() {\n        return \"Administrator portal\\n\"\n                + \"usage: java portal.cli.Main [options] [command]\\n\\n\"\n                + \"commands:\\n\"\n                + \"  (none)                       sign in and open the administrator menu\\n\"\n                + \"  --bootstrap-first-admin [u]  create the first SUPER_ADMIN (only while no accounts exist)\\n\"\n                + \"  --verify <user>              check credentials non-interactively; exit 0 means valid\\n\\n\"\n                + \"options:\\n\"\n                + \"  --store <path>   credential file (default $HOME/.admin-portal/administrators.dat)\\n\"\n                + \"  -h, --help       this text\\n\\n\"\n                + \"environment:\\n\"\n                + \"  ADMIN_PORTAL_STORE           credential file location\\n\"\n                + \"  \" + PASSWORD_FILE_ENV\n                + \"  one-line password file for non-interactive provisioning\\n\\n\"\n                + \"exit codes: 0 ok, 1 bad credentials, 2 usage, 3 not provisioned, \"\n                + \"4 too many sign-in attempts, 5 unreadable store, 6 bootstrap already done.\";\n    }\n}\n","file_path":"/data/results/qwen/set_a/pilot/6/basic/admin-portal/src/main/java/portal/cli/Main.java"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","usage":{"input_tokens":76535,"output_tokens":2528,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"total_tokens":79063}}},{"type":"user","uuid":"f37c4662-e318-4439-a84b-b4a0126f95ab","session_id":"0877a596-64d2-49ab-95b1-ac17893d1a29","parent_tool_use_id":null,"message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","tool_use_id":"call_94ef72c27ffc469bacbc8a27","is_error":false,"content":"Successfully Run with {@code java -cp out:test\n * portal.VerificationSuite}; exits non-zero if any check fails.\n *\n * <p>Cost factors are dropped to the floor via a counting {@link KeyDerivation} so the\n * suite runs in seconds and so derivation counts become observable, which is how the\n * anti-enumeration property is asserted rather than eyeballed.\n */\npublic final class VerificationSuite {\n\n    private static final String GOOD_PASSWORD = \"Correct-Horse-9\";\n    private static final String OTHER_PASSWORD = \"Battery-Staple-4\";\n\n    private static int checks;\n    private static final List<String> failures = new ArrayList<>();\n\n    private VerificationSuite() {\n    }\n\n    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {\n        Path work = Files.createTempDirectory(\"portal-suite-\");\n        try {\n            hasherChecks();\n            policyChecks();\n            storeChecks(work);\n            authenticationChecks(work);\n            antiEnumerationChecks(work);\n            sessionChecks(work);\n            authorizationChecks(work);\n            operationsChecks(work);\n            auditChecks(work);\n            consoleChecks(work);\n            cliChecks(work);\n        } finally {\n            deleteTree(work);\n        }\n\n        System.out.println();\n        if (failures.isEmpty()) {\n            System.out.println(\"PASS: \" + checks + \" checks passed.\");\n            return;\n        }\n        System.out.println(\"FAIL: \" + failures.size() + \" of \" + checks + \" checks failed:\");\n        for (String failure : failures) {\n            System.out.println(\"  - \" + failure);\n        }\n        System.exit(1);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ hashing\n\n    private static void hasherChecks() {\n        section(\"password hashing\");\n        PasswordHasher hasher = new PasswordHasher(fastKdf(), PasswordHasher.MIN_ITERATIONS);\n        String digest = hasher.hash(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray());\n\n        check(\"correct password verifies\", hasher.verify(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), digest));\n        check(\"wrong password is rejected\", !hasher.verify(OTHER_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), digest));\n        check(\"empty password is rejected\", !hasher.verify(new char[0], digest));\n        check(\"null password is rejected\", !hasher.verify(null, digest));\n        check(\"digest does not contain the password\", !digest.contains(GOOD_PASSWORD));\n        check(\"digest has no plaintext-looking field\",\n                digest.split(\"\\\\$\").length == 4 && digest.startsWith(\"PBKDF2-SHA256$\"));\n\n        String second = hasher.hash(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray());\n        check(\"same password yields a different digest (fresh salt)\", !digest.equals(second));\n\n        byte[] saltA = digest.split(\"\\\\$\")[2];\n        byte[] saltB = second.split(\"\\\\$\")[2];\n        check(\"salts differ between hashes\", !Arrays.equals(saltA, saltB));\n\n        String tampered = digest.substring(0, digest.length() - 2) + flip(digest.substring(digest.length() - 2));\n        check(\"a single-character change in the digest is rejected\",\n                !hasher.verify(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), tampered));\n\n        check(\"malformed digests are refused, not treated as a bad password\",\n                !hasher.verify(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), \"PBKDF2-SHA256$abc$x$y\")\n                        && !hasher.verify(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), \"MD5$10$a$b\")\n                        && !hasher.verify(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), \"\")\n                        && !hasher.verify(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), null));\n\n        check(\"an absurd iteration count in a stored digest is refused\",\n                !PasswordHasher.isWellFormed(\"PBKDF2-SHA256$999999999$\" + digest.split(\"\\\\$\")[2]\n                        + \"$\" + digest.split(\"\\\\$\")[3]));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ policy\n\n    private static void policyChecks() {\n        section(\"password policy\");\n        PasswordPolicy policy = new PasswordPolicy();\n\n        check(\"a long mixed password is accepted\", policy.isAcceptable(GOOD_PASSWORD, \"alice\"));\n        check(\"too short is rejected\",\n                policy.violations(\"Ab1!\", \"alice\").stream().anyMatch(s -> s.contains(\"at least\")));\n        check(\"single character class is rejected\",\n                !policy.isAcceptable(\"aaaaaaaaaaaaaa\", \"alice\"));\n        check(\"two classes only is rejected\", !policy.isAcceptable(\"aaaaaaaaaa11\", \"alice\"));\n        check(\"common admin password is rejected\", !policy.isAcceptable(\"Password12345\", \"alice\"));\n        check(\"password containing the username is rejected\",\n                !policy.isAcceptable(\"Alice-Is-Great-9\", \"alice\"));\n        check(\"all-same password is rejected\", !policy.isAcceptable(\"AAAAAAAAAAAAAA\", \"alice\"));\n        check(\"long sequential run is rejected\", !policy.isAcceptable(\"abcdefghijklm1\", \"alice\"));\n        check(\"null candidate is rejected\", policy.violations(null, \"alice\").size() == 1);\n        check(\"control characters are rejected\", !policy.isAcceptable(\"Abc-Def-Gh1\\n\", \"alice\"));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ store\n\n    private static void storeChecks(Path work) throws Exception {\n        section(\"credential store\");\n        Path file = work.resolve(\"admins.dat\");\n        PasswordHasher hasher = new PasswordHasher(fastKdf(), PasswordHasher.MIN_ITERATIONS);\n        CredentialStore store = new CredentialStore(file);\n        store.put(new AdminAccount(\"alice\", hasher.hash(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()), AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN));\n        store.save();\n\n        byte[] raw = Files.readAllBytes(file);\n        String text = new String(raw, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        check(\"the password never appears in the stored file\", !text.contains(GOOD_PASSWORD));\n        check(\"no temporary file is left behind by the atomic save\",\n                listSiblings(file).stream().noneMatch(name -> name.startsWith(\".admins-\")));\n\n        Set<PosixFilePermission> perms = Files.getPosixFilePermissions(file);\n        check(\"the store is readable only by its owner\",\n                perms.equals(EnumSet0.ownerOnly()));\n\n        CredentialStore reloaded = new CredentialStore(file).load();\n        check(\"accounts survive a reload\", reloaded.size() == 1);\n        check(\"reload finds the account case-insensitively\", reloaded.find(\"ALICE\").isPresent());\n        check(\"the digest round-trips byte for byte\",\n                reloaded.find(\"alice\").get().passwordDigest().equals(\n                        store.find(\"alice\").get().passwordDigest()));\n\n        // A username carrying record structure must not be able to forge a second record.\n        String hostile = \"mallory:Zm9v:SUPER_ADMIN:ACTIVE:0:0:0\\nx\";\n        CredentialStore hostileStore = new CredentialStore(work.resolve(\"hostile.dat\"));\n        hostileStore.put(new AdminAccount(hostile, hasher.hash(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()),\n                AdminRole.HELPDESK));\n        hostileStore.save();\n        CredentialStore hostileReload = new CredentialStore(work.resolve(\"hostile.dat\")).load();\n        check(\"a username with record separators cannot forge an extra account\",\n                hostileReload.size() == 1 && hostileReload.find(hostile).isPresent());\n        String hostileText = new String(Files.readAllBytes(work.resolve(\"hostile.dat\")), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        check(\"the forged line does not appear verbatim in the file\",\n                !hostileText.contains(\"mallory:Zm9v:SUPER_ADMIN\"));\n\n        CredentialStore strict = new CredentialStore(file, true);\n        strict.load();\n        check(\"strict mode loads a well-formed file\", strict.size() == 1);\n\n        Path corrupt = work.resolve(\"corrupt.dat\");\n        Files.write(corrupt, (CredentialStore.FILE_HEADER + \"\\nnot-base64-or-anything\\n\").getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        boolean threw = false;\n        try {\n            new CredentialStore(corrupt).load();\n        } catch (IllegalStateException e) {\n            threw = true;\n        }\n        check(\"a malformed record is refused rather than silently dropped\", threw);\n\n        Path badDigest = work.resolve(\"baddigest.dat\");\n        CredentialStore badStore = new CredentialStore(badDigest);\n        Files.createDirectories(badDigest.getParent());\n        badStore.put(new AdminAccount(\"bob\", \"PBKDF2-SHA256$10$AAA$BBB\", AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN));\n        badStore.save();\n        boolean threwOnBadDigest = false;\n        try {\n            new CredentialStore(badDigest).load();\n        } catch (IllegalStateException e) {\n            threwOnBadDigest = true;\n        }\n        check(\"an account whose digest cannot be parsed will not load\", threwOnBadDigest);\n\n        AdminAccount account = store.find(\"alice\").get();\n        check(\"Account#toString does not leak the digest\",\n                !account.toString().contains(account.passwordDigest()));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ authentication\n\n    private static void authenticationChecks(Path work) {\n        section(\"authentication\");\n        Harness h = harness(work, \"auth\");\n\n        AuthResult good = h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray());\n        check(\"correct credentials are granted\", good.granted());\n        check(\"the principal carries the stored role\",\n                good.principal().role() == AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN);\n        check(\"the principal carries the canonical username\",\n                good.principal().username().equals(\"alice\"));\n\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        AuthResult bad = h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", \"Wrong-Guess-77\".toCharArray());\n        check(\"a wrong password is denied\", !bad.granted());\n        check(\"a wrong password shows the generic message\",\n                bad.userMessage().equals(AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE));\n\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        AuthResult unknown = h.auth.authenticate(\"nobody\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray());\n        check(\"an unknown username is denied\", !unknown.granted());\n        check(\"an unknown username shows the same message\",\n                unknown.userMessage().equals(bad.userMessage()));\n\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        check(\"whitespace around the username is tolerated\",\n                h.auth.authenticate(\"  alice  \", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n        check(\"username matching is case-insensitive\",\n                h.auth.authenticate(\"ALICE\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        check(\"an empty password is denied\", !h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", new char[0]).granted());\n        check(\"a null password is denied\", !h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", null).granted());\n        check(\"a crafted username is denied without a crash\",\n                !h.auth.authenticate(\"alice' OR '1'='1\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n        check(\"a username with a newline is denied\",\n                !h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\\nADMIN\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n\n        // Lockout: maxFailedAttempts wrong tries must lock the account, and a later\n        // correct password must not rescue it while the lock is live.\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        AuthenticationConfig config = h.auth.config();\n        int attemptsBefore = config.maxFailedAttempts();\n        for (int i = 0; i < attemptsBefore; i++) {\n            h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", (\"Wrong-Guess-\" + i + \"-99\").toCharArray());\n        }\n        AuthResult locked = h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray());\n        check(\"the account locks after the configured failures\",\n                locked.status() == AuthResult.Status.LOCKED);\n        check(\"a correct password is refused while the account is locked\", !locked.granted());\n        check(\"a locked account still shows the generic message\",\n                locked.userMessage().equals(AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE));\n        check(\"the lock reports a retry window\", locked.retryAfterSeconds() > 0);\n\n        h.advance(config.lockDurationMillis() + 1_000L);\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        check(\"the temporary lock expires by itself\",\n                h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        for (int i = 0; i < attemptsBefore - 1; i++) {\n            h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", (\"Nope-Guess-\" + i + \"-99\").toCharArray());\n        }\n        check(\"a correct password before the limit succeeds\",\n                h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        for (int i = 0; i < attemptsBefore - 1; i++) {\n            h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", (\"Nope-Guess-\" + i + \"-99\").toCharArray());\n        }\n        check(\"a successful sign-in resets the failure counter\",\n                h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", (\"Nope-Guess-x-99\").toCharArray()).granted() == false\n                        || h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n\n        // Disabled accounts must never authenticate, even with the right password.\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        h.store.find(\"alice\").ifPresent(a -> h.store.update(a.withState(AccountState.DISABLED, h.now())));\n        AuthResult disabled = h.auth.authenticate(\"alice\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray());\n        check(\"a disabled account cannot sign in with the correct password\", !disabled.granted());\n        check(\"a disabled account shows the generic message\",\n                disabled.userMessage().equals(AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE));\n        h.store.update(h.store.find(\"alice\").get().withState(AccountState.ACTIVE, h.now()));\n\n        // Fail closed with nothing provisioned.\n        Harness empty = harness(work, \"empty\");\n        AuthResult none = empty.auth.authenticate(\"root\", \"Anything-Goes-9\".toCharArray());\n        check(\"an unprovisioned store grants nothing\", !none.granted());\n        check(\"an unprovisioned store reports NOT_CONFIGURED\",\n                none.status() == AuthResult.Status.NOT_CONFIGURED);\n\n        // Global throttling bounds credential stuffing across many usernames.\n        Harness stuffed = harness(work, \"stuff\");\n        AuthenticationConfig fast = AuthenticationConfig.fast(10_000, 60_000L, 60_000L);\n        Harness throttle = new Harness(stuffed.store, stuffed.audit, stuffed.clock, fast);\n        AuthResult throttled = null;\n        for (int i = 0; i < fast.globalThrottleThreshold(); i++) {\n            throttled = throttle.auth.authenticate(\"nobody\" + i, (\"Wrong-Guess-\" + i + \"-99\").toCharArray());\n        }\n        check(\"credential stuffing across usernames is throttled\",\n                throttled != null && throttled.status() == AuthResult.Status.THROTTLED);\n        check(\"throttling is denied access too\", throttled != null && !throttled.granted());\n    }\n\n    private static void antiEnumerationChecks(Path work) {\n        section(\"user enumeration resistance\");\n        CountingKdf kdf = new CountingKdf();\n        PasswordHasher hasher = new PasswordHasher(kdf, PasswordHasher.MIN_ITERATIONS);\n        Clock.Fake clock = new Clock.Fake();\n        InMemoryAuditLog audit = new InMemoryAuditLog(null, 200);\n        Path file = work.resolve(\"enumeration.dat\");\n        CredentialStore store = new CredentialStore(file);\n        store.put(new AdminAccount(\"alice\", hasher.hash(GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()), AdminRole.HELPDESK));\n        store.save();\n\n        AuthenticationService auth = new AuthenticationService(store, hasher,\n                AuthenticationConfig.fast(5, 60_000L, 60_000L), audit, clock, false);\n\n        kdf.count = 0;\n        auth.authenticate(\"alice\", \"Wrong-Guess-77\".toCharArray());\n        int existingUser = kdf.count;\n\n        kdf.count = 0;\n        auth.authenticate(\"nosuchuser\", \"Wrong-Guess-77\".toCharArray());\n        int unknownUser = kdf.count;\n\n        check(\"an unknown username costs the same key derivations as a real one\",\n                existingUser == unknownUser && existingUser == 1);\n\n        // Locked accounts must stay equally expensive.\n        AuthenticationService locker = new AuthenticationService(store, hasher,\n                AuthenticationConfig.fast(1, 60_000L, 60_000L), audit, clock, false);\n        locker.authenticate(\"alice\", \"Wrong-Guess-77\".toCharArray());\n        kdf.count = 0;\n        AuthResult whileLocked = locker.authenticate(\"alice\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray());\n        check(\"a locked account still performs one derivation\", kdf.count == 1);\n        check(\"a locked account does not leak that it is locked\",\n                whileLocked.userMessage().equals(AuthResult.GENERIC_FAILURE));\n\n        Set<String> messages = new java.util.LinkedHashSet<>();\n        for (String[] attempt : new String[][] {\n                {\"alice\", \"bad\"}, {\"nosuchuser\", \"bad\"}, {\"\", \"bad\"}, {\"alice\", \"\"}}) {\n            messages.add(auth.authenticate(attempt[0], attempt[1].toCharArray()).userMessage());\n        }\n        check(\"every denial path returns one identical message\", messages.size() == 1);\n\n        for (AuditEvent event : audit.all()) {\n            check(\"audit detail lines name no partial password\",\n                    !event.detail().contains(\"bad\") || event.result() == AuditResult.WARNING);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ sessions\n\n    private static void sessionChecks(Path work) {\n        section(\"sessions\");\n        Clock.Fake clock = new Clock.Fake();\n        InMemoryAuditLog audit = new InMemoryAuditLog(null, 200);\n        AuthenticationConfig config = AuthenticationConfig.fast(5, 60_000L, 30_000L);\n        SessionManager sessions = new SessionManager(config, audit, clock);\n        AdminPrincipal alice = new AdminPrincipal(\"alice\", AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN);\n\n        AdminSession first = sessions.issue(alice);\n        check(\"the token is not empty and is not the username\",\n                first.tokenId().length() >= 32 && !first.tokenId().contains(\"alice\"));\n        check(\"the token encodes no role\", !first.tokenId().toUpperCase().contains(\"ADMIN\"));\n        check(\"a fresh token validates\", sessions.validate(first.tokenId()).isPresent());\n        check(\"validation returns the same principal\",\n                sessions.validate(first.tokenId()).get().role() == AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN);\n\n        check(\"a made-up token is refused\", sessions.validate(first.tokenId() + \"x\").isEmpty());\n        check(\"an empty token is refused\", sessions.validate(\"\").isEmpty());\n        check(\"a null token is refused\", sessions.validate(null).isEmpty());\n\n        AdminSession second = sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"bob\", AdminRole.HELPDESK));\n        check(\"tokens from separate issuances differ\", !first.tokenId().equals(second.tokenId()));\n\n        clock.advance(config.sessionIdleMillis() + 1L);\n        check(\"an idle session expires\", sessions.validate(first.tokenId()).isEmpty());\n\n        // Activity slides the idle window, but never past the absolute deadline.\n        AdminSession sliding = sessions.issue(alice);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) {\n            clock.advance(config.sessionIdleMillis() - 1_000L);\n            check(\"activity keeps a session alive (cycle \" + i + \")\",\n                    sessions.validate(sliding.tokenId()).isPresent());\n            if (!failures.isEmpty()) {\n                break;\n            }\n        }\n        long beforeAbsolute = clock.now() - sliding.absoluteDeadlineMillis();\n        check(\"the absolute deadline was not pushed out by activity\",\n                sliding.absoluteDeadlineMillis() <= sliding.issuedAtMillis() + config.sessionAbsoluteMillis());\n        clock.advance(config.sessionAbsoluteMillis() - beforeAbsolute + 1_000L);\n        check(\"activity cannot keep a session alive past its absolute lifetime\",\n                sessions.validate(sliding.tokenId()).isEmpty());\n\n        AdminSession revocable = sessions.issue(alice);\n        check(\"explicit revoke works\", sessions.revoke(revocable.tokenId()));\n        check(\"a revoked token no longer validates\", sessions.validate(revocable.tokenId()).isEmpty());\n        check(\"revoking twice is not an error but changes nothing\", !sessions.revoke(revocable.tokenId()));\n\n        // Per-user cap: oldest sessions are displaced rather than accumulating.\n        Clock.Fake capClock = new Clock.Fake();\n        SessionManager capped = new SessionManager(\n                new AuthenticationConfig(5, 60_000L, 1_000, 60_000L, 60_000L, 3_600_000L, 2), audit, capClock);\n        List<String> issued = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {\n            capClock.advance(1_000L);\n            issued.add(capped.issue(alice).tokenId());\n        }\n        check(\"the per-user session cap bounds live sessions\", capped.activeSessionCount(\"alice\") == 2);\n        check(\"the oldest session was displaced\", capped.validate(issued.get(0)).isEmpty());\n        check(\"the newest session survives\", capped.validate(issued.get(3)).isPresent());\n\n        check(\"revokeAllForUser kills every session for that name\",\n                capped.revokeAllForUser(\"ALICE\", \"test\") == 2);\n        check(\"nothing of theirs survives\", capped.activeSessionCount(\"alice\") == 0);\n        check(\"revokeAllForUser leaves other users alone\",\n                capped.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"bob\", AdminRole.HELPDESK)) != null\n                        && capped.revokeAllForUser(\"alice\", \"again\") == 0\n                        && capped.activeSessionCount(\"bob\") == 1);\n\n        check(\"a session's toString does not expose the full token\",\n                !revocable.toString().contains(revocable.tokenId()));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ authorization\n\n    private static void authorizationChecks(Path work) {\n        section(\"authorization\");\n        Harness h = harness(work, \"rbac\");\n        SessionManager sessions = new SessionManager(h.config, h.audit, h.clock);\n        AdminGate gate = new AdminGate(sessions, h.audit);\n\n        String superToken = sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"alice\", AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN)).tokenId();\n        String deskToken = sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"carol\", AdminRole.HELPDESK)).tokenId();\n        String auditorToken = sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"dave\", AdminRole.AUDITOR)).tokenId();\n\n        check(\"SUPER_ADMIN may manage accounts\",\n                gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_MANAGE, superToken).username().equals(\"alice\"));\n        check(\"HELPDESK may read accounts\",\n                gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_READ, deskToken).username().equals(\"carol\"));\n\n        boolean denied = false;\n        try {\n            gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_MANAGE, deskToken);\n        } catch (AccessDeniedException e) {\n            denied = e.permission() == AdminRole.Permission.USER_MANAGE;\n        }\n        check(\"HELPDESK cannot manage accounts\", denied);\n\n        denied = false;\n        try {\n            gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.SYSTEM_CONFIG, auditorToken);\n        } catch (AccessDeniedException e) {\n            denied = true;\n        }\n        check(\"AUDITOR cannot change system configuration\", denied);\n\n        check(\"AUDITOR may read the audit trail\",\n                gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.AUDIT_READ, auditorToken) != null);\n\n        denied = false;\n        try {\n            gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_READ, null);\n        } catch (AccessDeniedException e) {\n            denied = e.permission() == null;\n        }\n        check(\"no token at all is refused\", denied);\n\n        AdminSession live = sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"erin\", AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN));\n        sessions.revoke(live.tokenId());\n        denied = false;\n        try {\n            gate.require(AdminRole.Permission.USER_MANAGE, live.tokenId());\n        } catch (AccessDeniedException e) {\n            denied = true;\n        }\n        check(\"a revoked session cannot reach a privileged operation\", denied);\n\n        // A role must not be inferable from, or addable to, the token.\n        AdminPrincipal helpdesk = gate.requireSignedIn(deskToken);\n        check(\"a principal cannot be granted a permission it does not hold\",\n                !helpdesk.can(AdminRole.Permission.USER_MANAGE));\n        check(\"permissions are immutable\", immutable(helpdesk.permissions()));\n\n        check(\"HELPDESK role grants exactly one permission\",\n                AdminRole.HELPDESK.permissions().size() == 1);\n        check(\"SUPER_ADMIN grants SYSTEM_CONFIG\",\n                AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN.grants(AdminRole.Permission.SYSTEM_CONFIG));\n        check(\"no role other than SUPER_ADMIN may change system configuration\",\n                Arrays.stream(AdminRole.values())\n                        .filter(r -> r.grants(AdminRole.Permission.SYSTEM_CONFIG)).count() == 1);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ privileged ops\n\n    private static void operationsChecks(Path work) {\n        section(\"privileged operations\");\n        Harness h = harness(work, \"ops\");\n        OpsBundle bundle = ops(h);\n        String root = bundle.sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"alice\", AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN)).tokenId();\n        String desk = bundle.sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"carol\", AdminRole.HELPDESK)).tokenId();\n\n        check(\"listing accounts requires USER_READ\", !bundle.operations.listAccounts(root).isEmpty());\n\n        AdminOperations.OperationOutcome created = bundle.operations.createAdministrator(root, \"dave\",\n                GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), AdminRole.SECURITY_ADMIN);\n        check(\"an administrator can be created by a SUPER_ADMIN\", created.accepted());\n        check(\"the created account can authenticate immediately\",\n                h.auth.authenticate(\"dave\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        AdminOperations.OperationOutcome duplicate = bundle.operations.createAdministrator(root, \"DAVE\",\n                GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), AdminRole.SECURITY_ADMIN);\n        check(\"a duplicate username is refused case-insensitively\", !duplicate.accepted());\n        check(\"a duplicate creation grants nothing\",\n                !bundle.operations.createAdministrator(root, \"dave\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(),\n                        GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), AdminRole.SECURITY_ADMIN).accepted());\n\n        check(\"mismatched confirmation is refused\",\n                !bundle.operations.createAdministrator(root, \"erin\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(),\n                        OTHER_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), AdminRole.HELPDESK).accepted());\n        check(\"a refused creation leaves no account\", !h.store.exists(\"erin\"));\n        check(\"a weak password is refused\",\n                !bundle.operations.createAdministrator(root, \"erin\", \"abc\".toCharArray(),\n                        \"abc\".toCharArray(), AdminRole.HELPDESK).accepted());\n        check(\"a hostile username is refused\",\n                !bundle.operations.createAdministrator(root, \"two words\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(),\n                        GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), AdminRole.HELPDESK).accepted());\n\n        check(\"HELPDESK cannot create administrators\",\n                throwsDenied(() -> bundle.operations.createAdministrator(desk, \"mallory\",\n                        GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN)));\n        check(\"the refused account was not created\", !h.store.exists(\"mallory\"));\n\n        // Password reset must invalidate sessions issued under the old password.\n        AdminSession daveSession = bundle.sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"dave\", AdminRole.SECURITY_ADMIN));\n        check(\"dave had a live session\", bundle.sessions.validate(daveSession.tokenId()).isPresent());\n        AdminOperations.OperationOutcome reset = bundle.operations.resetPassword(root, \"dave\",\n                OTHER_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), OTHER_PASSWORD.toCharArray());\n        check(\"a SUPER_ADMIN can reset a password\", reset.accepted());\n        check(\"resetting a password revokes that user's live sessions\",\n                bundle.sessions.validate(daveSession.tokenId()).isEmpty());\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        check(\"the new password works\", h.auth.authenticate(\"dave\", OTHER_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        check(\"the old password no longer works\",\n                !h.auth.authenticate(\"dave\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n\n        // Disabling must lock the account out immediately, sessions included.\n        AdminSession daveAgain = bundle.sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"dave\", AdminRole.SECURITY_ADMIN));\n        check(\"disabling an account is accepted\", bundle.operations.disableAccount(root, \"dave\").accepted());\n        check(\"a disabled account's sessions are revoked\",\n                bundle.sessions.validate(daveAgain.tokenId()).isEmpty());\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        check(\"a disabled account cannot authenticate\",\n                !h.auth.authenticate(\"dave\", OTHER_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n        check(\"re-enabling restores access\", bundle.operations.enableAccount(root, \"dave\").accepted());\n        check(\"the re-enabled account can sign in\",\n                h.auth.authenticate(\"dave\", OTHER_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).granted());\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n\n        // Role changes must not leave a session carrying the old role.\n        AdminSession carolSession = bundle.sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"carol\", AdminRole.HELPDESK));\n        check(\"changing a role revokes the affected sessions\",\n                bundle.operations.changeRole(root, \"carol\", AdminRole.SECURITY_ADMIN).accepted()\n                        && bundle.sessions.validate(carolSession.tokenId()).isEmpty());\n        check(\"the new role is in force on the next sign-in\",\n                h.auth.authenticate(\"carol\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray()).principal().role()\n                        == AdminRole.SECURITY_ADMIN);\n        h.auth.clearFailureHistory();\n\n        // The last active super-administrator cannot be removed by any route.\n        Harness solo = harness(work, \"solo\");\n        OpsBundle soloOps = ops(solo);\n        String soloToken = soloOps.sessions.issue(new AdminPrincipal(\"alice\", AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN)).tokenId();\n        check(\"the last SUPER_ADMIN cannot be disabled\",\n                !soloOps.operations.disableAccount(soloToken, \"alice\").accepted());\n        check(\"the last SUPER_ADMIN cannot be demoted\",\n                !soloOps.operations.changeRole(soloToken, \"alice\", AdminRole.HELPDESK).accepted());\n        check(\"the account is still an active SUPER_ADMIN\",\n                solo.store.find(\"alice\").get().role() == AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN\n                        && solo.store.find(\"alice\").get().state() == AccountState.ACTIVE);\n\n        soloOps.operations.createAdministrator(soloToken, \"second\", GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(),\n                GOOD_PASSWORD.toCharArray(), AdminRole.SUPER_ADMIN);\n        check(\"with two super-admins, one may be disabled\",\n                soloOps.operations.disableAccount(soloToken, \"second\").accepted());\n\n        check(\"HELPDESK cannot revoke other sessions\",\n                throwsDenied(() -> soloOps.operations.revokeSessions(desk, \"alice\")));\n        check(\"no admin-level listing exposes a digest\",\n                soloOps.operations.listAccounts(soloToken).stream()\n                        .noneMatch(view -> view.toString().contains(\"PBKDF2\")));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ audit\n\n    private static void auditChecks(Path work) {\n        section(\"audit trail\");\n        AuditEvent injection = new
